## Call 1

```python
import numpy as np
print(sorted(W.keys()))
for k in sorted(W.keys()):
    a=W[k]
    print(k,a.shape,a.dtype,float(np.std(a)),float(np.abs(a).max()), float(np.mean(a)))
for l in range(4):
    for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v','W_o']:
        M=W[f'{nm}.{l}']
        s=np.linalg.svd(M,compute_uv=False)
        print(l,nm,'fro',np.linalg.norm(M).round(2),'top sv',s[:6].round(2),'median',np.median(s).round(3),'min',s[-1].round(4))
    for nm in ['W_fc','W_proj']:
        M=W[f'{nm}.{l}']
        s=np.linalg.svd(M,compute_uv=False)
        print(l,nm,'fro',np.linalg.norm(M).round(2),'top sv',s[:6].round(2),'median',np.median(s).round(3),'min',s[-1].round(4))
    print('ln1_w',W[f'ln1_w.{l}'].mean(),W[f'ln1_w.{l}'].std(),'ln1_b',W[f'ln1_b.{l}'].mean(),W[f'ln1_b.{l}'].std())
    print('ln2_w',W[f'ln2_w.{l}'].mean(),W[f'ln2_w.{l}'].std(),'ln2_b',W[f'ln2_b.{l}'].mean(),W[f'ln2_b.{l}'].std())

```

## Call 2

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
for l in range(4):
    for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v','W_o','W_fc','W_proj']:
        M=W[f'{nm}.{l}']
        s=np.linalg.svd(M,compute_uv=False)
        print(l,nm,s[:60])
        print('   count>1:',(s>1).sum(),'>2:',(s>2).sum(),'>0.8',(s>0.8).sum())
# per head spectra
for l in range(4):
    for j in range(6):
        out=[]
        for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
            M=W[f'{nm}.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
            s=np.linalg.svd(M,compute_uv=False)
            out.append((nm,np.round(s[:5],1),int((s>0.8).sum()),round(float(np.linalg.norm(M)),1)))
        M=W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
        s=np.linalg.svd(M,compute_uv=False)
        out.append(('W_o',np.round(s[:5],1),int((s>0.5).sum()),round(float(np.linalg.norm(M)),1)))
        print(l,j,out)

```

## Call 3

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=3,suppress=True)
# token-subspace candidate: top right singular vectors of W_v.0
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
print('W_v.0 sv 20..45',s[20:45])
for r in [28,30]:
    T=Vt[:r].T
    print('--- r',r)
    for j in range(6):
        row=[]
        for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
            M=W[f'{nm}.0'][64*j:64*j+64]
            row.append((nm,round(float((np.linalg.norm(M@T)/np.linalg.norm(M))**2),3)))
        print('L0H',j,row)
# symmetric / antisymmetric split of the bilinear form, and eigenvalue phases
for l in range(4):
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
        B=Q.T@K
        S=(B+B.T)/2; A=(B-B.T)/2
        ev=np.linalg.eigvals(B)
        ev=ev[np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))][:12]
        tr=np.trace(B)/ (np.linalg.norm(Q)*np.linalg.norm(K))
        print(f'L{l}H{j} sym {np.linalg.norm(S):7.2f} anti {np.linalg.norm(A):7.2f} ratio {np.linalg.norm(A)/np.linalg.norm(S):.2f} cos(Q,K)={tr:.3f} top |ev| {np.abs(ev[:4]).round(1)} phase(deg) {np.degrees(np.angle(ev[:8])).round(0)}')
# subspace overlap between heads in L0 (Q+K row spaces)
def rs(l,j,r=20):
    M=np.vstack([W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64],W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]])
    return np.linalg.svd(M,full_matrices=False)[2][:r].T
for l in range(4):
    O=np.zeros((6,6))
    for a in range(6):
        for b in range(6):
            O[a,b]=np.linalg.norm(rs(l,a).T@rs(l,b))**2/20
    print('L',l,'Q+K top20 subspace overlap');print(O)

```

## Call 4

```python
import numpy as np, scipy.linalg as sl
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
def B(l,j):
    Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
    return Q.T@K
for j in [1,5,0,2,3]:
    ev=np.linalg.eigvals(B(0,j)); ev=ev[np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))]
    keep=ev[(ev.imag>=0)][:32]
    print('L0H',j,'mag',np.abs(keep)); print('   phase',np.degrees(np.angle(keep)).round(0))
# restrict to complement of token space to isolate position-driven part
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
Pc=np.eye(384)-T@T.T
print('--- after projecting token subspace out')
for j in [1,5,0,2,3]:
    b=Pc@B(0,j)@Pc
    ev=np.linalg.eigvals(b); ev=ev[np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))]
    keep=ev[(ev.imag>=0)][:32]
    print('L0H',j,'fro',round(float(np.linalg.norm(b)),1),'of',round(float(np.linalg.norm(B(0,j))),1),'mag',np.abs(keep)); print('   phase',np.degrees(np.angle(keep)).round(0))
print('--- token-token block')
for j in range(6):
    b=T.T@B(0,j)@T
    ev=np.linalg.eigvals(b); ev=ev[np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))]
    print('L0H',j,'fro',round(float(np.linalg.norm(b)),2),'trace',round(float(np.trace(b)),2),'top ev',ev[:6].round(2))
# how much do H1 and H5 commute?
for a,b_ in [(1,5),(0,5),(2,3),(1,2),(5,2)]:
    A=B(0,a);C=B(0,b_)
    print('commutator rel',a,b_,round(float(np.linalg.norm(A@C-C@A)/(2*np.linalg.norm(A)*np.linalg.norm(C))),4))
```

```python
import numpy as np, scipy.linalg as sl
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=220,precision=2,suppress=True)
def B(l,j):
    Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
    return Q.T@K
B5=B(0,5);B1=B(0,1)
print('sv B5',np.linalg.svd(B5,compute_uv=False)[:30])
print('sv B1',np.linalg.svd(B1,compute_uv=False)[:30])
ev,V=np.linalg.eig(B5)
o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)); ev=ev[o];V=V[:,o]
keep=[i for i in range(40) if ev[i].imag>=0][:16]
Vk=V[:,keep]
print('eigvec overlap |v_a^H v_b|');print(np.abs(Vk.conj().T@Vk))
print('idx mag phase | H1 rho mag phase | H0 | H2 | H3 mag phase')
for i in keep:
    v=V[:,i]
    line=[f'{abs(ev[i]):6.2f} {np.degrees(np.angle(ev[i])):6.0f}']
    for j in [1,0,2,3]:
        r=v.conj()@B(0,j)@v
        line.append(f'{abs(r):6.2f} {np.degrees(np.angle(r)):6.0f}')
    print(' | '.join(line))
print('Same from H1 eigenvectors')
ev1,V1=np.linalg.eig(B1)
o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev1)); ev1=ev1[o];V1=V1[:,o]
keep=[i for i in range(40) if ev1[i].imag>=0][:14]
for i in keep:
    v=V1[:,i]
    r=v.conj()@B5@v
    print(f'H1 {abs(ev1[i]):6.2f} {np.degrees(np.angle(ev1[i])):6.0f} | H5 rho {abs(r):6.2f} {np.degrees(np.angle(r)):6.0f}')
```

## Call 6

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=220,precision=2,suppress=True)
def B(l,j):
    Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
    return Q.T@K
B5=B(0,5);B1=B(0,1)
ev,V=np.linalg.eig(B5)
o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)); ev=ev[o];V=V[:,o]
# orthonormal basis of the joint position subspace via H1+H5 rows
M=np.vstack([W['W_q.0'][64:128],W['W_k.0'][64:128],W['W_q.0'][320:384],W['W_k.0'][320:384]])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(M,full_matrices=False)
print('joint sv',s[:60])
r=int((s>1.0).sum()); print('r',r)
E=vt[:r].T
b5=E.T@B5@E; b1=E.T@B1@E
# real Schur style: eigenvectors of normal part of b5 + eps b1 to get shared planes
ev5,V5=np.linalg.eig(b5)
o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev5)); ev5=ev5[o];V5=V5[:,o]
modes=[]
for i in range(r):
    if ev5[i].imag<-1e-6: continue
    v=V5[:,i]
    g5=ev5[i]; g1=v.conj()@b1@v
    w=np.degrees(np.angle(g5))
    modes.append((abs(g5),w,abs(g1),np.degrees(np.angle(g1))))
modes=np.array(modes)
print(modes[:26])
def kern(mag,ph,om,ds):
    return np.array([np.sum(mag*np.cos(np.radians(om*d-ph))) for d in ds])
m=modes[modes[:,0]>1.0]
ds=np.arange(0,14)
for dstar in [1,2,3]:
    om=m[:,1]/dstar
    for equal in [False,True]:
        s5=kern(np.ones(len(m)) if equal else m[:,0],m[:,1],om,ds)
        s1=kern(np.ones(len(m)) if equal else m[:,2],m[:,3],om,ds)
        print('H5 offset assumed',dstar,'equal' if equal else 'weighted','s5',s5.round(1)); print('                     s1',s1.round(1))
```

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=220,precision=3,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
print('ln1_w.0 pct',np.percentile(w1,[0,5,25,50,75,95,100]));print('ln2_w.0 pct',np.percentile(w2,[0,5,25,50,75,95,100]))
print('corr w1,w2',np.corrcoef(w1,w2)[0,1])
# coordinate energy vs ln1 weights
for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
    e=(W[f'{nm}.0']**2).sum(0)
    print(nm,'corr energy vs w1',np.corrcoef(e,w1)[0,1])
e=(W['W_fc.0']**2).sum(0); print('fc energy vs w2',np.corrcoef(e,w2)[0,1])
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T          # token space in h0 coords
def Oh(l,j):
    return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
def orth(A,r=None):
    u,s,_=np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False)
    r=r or (s>s[0]*1e-2).sum()
    return u[:,:r],s
Tx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.03))@T                    # token space in x coords (rough)
subs={}
subs['tok']=orth(np.diag(w2)@Tx,30)[0]
for j in range(6):
    subs[f'H{j}out']=orth(np.diag(w2)@Oh(0,j)@T,28)[0]
pos=orth(np.vstack([W['W_q.0'][64:128],W['W_k.0'][64:128],W['W_q.0'][320:384],W['W_k.0'][320:384]]).T,40)[0]
subs['pos']=orth(np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.03))@pos,40)[0]
F=W['W_fc.0']
tot=np.linalg.norm(F)**2
for k,v in subs.items():
    print(k,'dim',v.shape[1],'fc energy frac',round(float(np.linalg.norm(F@v)**2/tot),3))
# mutual overlaps
names=list(subs)
O=np.array([[np.linalg.norm(subs[a].T@subs[b])**2/min(subs[a].shape[1],subs[b].shape[1]) for b in names] for a in names])
print(names);print(O)
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(F,full_matrices=False)
print('top fc right sv overlap with each subspace')
for i in range(8):
    print(i,round(float(s[i]),1),{k:round(float(np.linalg.norm(v.T@vt[i])**2),2) for k,v in subs.items()})
```

```python
import numpy as np

w1=W['ln1_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
Tx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.03))@T
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
sp={'cur':Tx}
for j in [5,1,0,2,3,4]: sp[f'H{j}']=Oh(0,j)@T
pos=np.linalg.svd(np.vstack([W['W_q.0'][64:128],W['W_k.0'][64:128],W['W_q.0'][320:384],W['W_k.0'][320:384]]),full_matrices=False)[2][:40].T
sp['pos']=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.03))@pos
def orth(A):
    return np.linalg.qr(A)[0]
print('fraction of head energy in each source subspace (after LN weight)')
for l in [1,2,3]:
    wl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    S={k:orth(np.diag(wl)@v) for k,v in sp.items()}
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64];K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64];V=W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
        f=lambda M:' '.join(f'{k}:{np.linalg.norm(M@v)**2/np.linalg.norm(M)**2:.2f}' for k,v in S.items())
        print(f'L{l}H{j} Q {f(Q)}');print(f'      K {f(K)}');print(f'      V {f(V)}')
print('\nsame-token match: trace(A^T Q^T K B)/fro, for query source A, key source B')
keys=['cur','H5','H1']
for l in [1,2,3]:
    wl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64];K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
        row=[]
        for a in keys:
            for b in keys:
                A=np.linalg.qr(np.diag(wl)@sp[a][:,:28])[0] if False else np.diag(wl)@sp[a][:,:28]
                C=np.diag(wl)@sp[b][:,:28]
                M=(Q@A).T@(K@C)
                row.append(f'{a}>{b}:{np.trace(M)/np.linalg.norm(M):+.2f}')
        print(f'L{l}H{j}',' '.join(row))
```

## Call 9

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=220,precision=2,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); 
for r in [27,28,30]:
    T=Vt[:r].T
    R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
    print('r',r,'R sv',np.linalg.svd(R,compute_uv=False)[:r])
    # kurtosis along raw principal axes vs ICA axes
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
    S=ica.fit_transform(R)
    k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sk=(S**3).mean(0)
    print(' ICA kurt sorted',np.sort(k)[::-1]); print(' skew',sk[np.argsort(-k)])
    print(' PCA kurt',np.sort(kurtosis(np.linalg.svd(R-R.mean(0),full_matrices=False)[0],axis=0))[::-1][:r])
    # sparsity of activity: neurons with big weight per component
    print(' active frac |S|>2',(np.abs(S)>2).mean(0)[np.argsort(-k)])

```

## Call 10

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=220,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
print('W_v.0 sv 24..40',s[24:40])
for r in [26,28,30,32,34,36,40,48]:
    T=Vt[:r].T
    R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=2e-5,random_state=1,fun='cube')
    S=ica.fit_transform(R)
    k=np.sort(kurtosis(S,axis=0))[::-1]
    print('r',r,'n kurt>30',(k>30).sum(),'n>20',(k>20).sum(),'tail',k[-max(8,r-24):])
# ICA on full W_fc.0
F=W['W_fc.0']
u,sf,vt=np.linalg.svd(F,full_matrices=False)
for r in [100,150,200]:
    X=F@vt[:r].T
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=2e-5,random_state=1,fun='cube')
    S=ica.fit_transform(X)
    k=kurtosis(S,axis=0)
    print('full r',r,'sorted kurt',np.sort(k)[::-1][:r//2],'n>30',(k>30).sum(),'n>10',(k>10).sum())

```

## Call 11

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=220,precision=2,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30
T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
axes=[]
for seed in [0,1,2]:
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed,fun='cube')
    S=ica.fit_transform(R)
    k=kurtosis(S,axis=0)
    S=S*np.sign((S**3).mean(0))
    o=np.argsort(-k); S=S[:,o]; axes.append(S)
    print('seed',seed,'kurt',k[o][:30].round(0))
C=np.abs(axes[0].T@axes[1])/1536
print('best match across seeds',C.max(1).round(2))
S=axes[0]
for c in range(30):
    a=np.sort(S[:,c])[::-1]
    print(c,'top',a[:7].round(1),'bottom',a[-3:].round(1),'n>3',(S[:,c]>3).sum(),'n<-3',(S[:,c]<-3).sum())
# co-activation between components
strong=S>3
co=(strong.T.astype(float)@strong.astype(float))
print(co.astype(int)[:12,:12])

```

## Call 12

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
import pickle
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
D=np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))
R=W['W_fc.0']@D@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0))
Um=ica.components_.T   # R-mean -> S : S=(R-m)@Um
o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(Um*sg)[:,o]; k=k[o]
Z=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)          # unit token coords
A=(R-R.mean(0))@Z                       # neuron activation per token (real units)
print('kurt',k.round(0))
print('rms activation per comp',A.std(0).round(2))
print('max',A.max(0).round(1))
print('sum of positive part',np.maximum(A,0).sum(0).round(0))
G=Z.T@Z
print('Gram of unit axes (x10)');print((G*10).round(0).astype(int))
np.save('/tmp/Z.npy',Z)

```

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
D=np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))
R=W['W_fc.0']@D@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z[:,:27]
A=(R-R.mean(0))@Z
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
Fw=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2)
slots={j:Fw@Oh(0,j)@T@Z for j in range(6)}
for j in range(6):
    a=slots[j]-slots[j].mean(0)
    print('slot H',j,'rms',a.std(0).round(2)[:27].mean().round(3),'kurt per token',kurtosis(a,axis=0).round(0))
# dual check: how sparse is each neuron over tokens (cur slot)?
Asc=A/A.std(0)
top=np.sort(np.abs(Asc),axis=1)
print('neurons with a token >4 sigma',(top[:,-1]>4).sum(),'of 1536')
# neurons strongly tuned to cur token c: average their H5-slot profile
a5=slots[5]/slots[5].std(0)
a1=slots[1]/slots[1].std(0)
P5=np.zeros((27,27));P1=np.zeros((27,27))
for c in range(27):
    m=Asc[:,c]>4
    P5[c]=a5[m].mean(0); P1[c]=a1[m].mean(0)
print('mean H5-slot profile of neurons tuned to cur token (rows=cur, cols=prev)')
print(P5.round(0).astype(int))

```

## Call 14

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]
# Token vectors are the columns t_c with R t_c = activation, so Z is already token coordinate in T.
tv=T@Z   # token vectors in h0 space (27)
for j in [0,2,3,1,5]:
    Q=W['W_q.0'][64*j:64*j+64];K=W['W_k.0'][64*j:64*j+64]
    qt=Q@tv;kt=K@tv
    uq,sq,vq=np.linalg.svd(qt-qt.mean(1,keepdims=True),full_matrices=False)
    uk,sk,vk=np.linalg.svd(kt-kt.mean(1,keepdims=True),full_matrices=False)
    print(f'H{j} q sv',sq[:6],' k sv',sk[:6])
    print('  q comp1',(vq[0]*sq[0]).round(1));print('  q comp2',(vq[1]*sq[1]).round(1))
    print('  k comp1',(vk[0]*sk[0]).round(1));print('  k comp2',(vk[1]*sk[1]).round(1))
for j in [3,2]:
    Q=W['W_q.0'][64*j:64*j+64];K=W['W_k.0'][64*j:64*j+64]
    M=(Q@tv).T@(K@tv)/8
    print(f'H{j} token-token score q row,k col (x1)');print(M.round(1))

```

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
P=W['W_proj.3']
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
print(s[:40])
for r in [24,26,28,30,32]:
    X=(u[:,:r].T@P).T   # neurons x r
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=5000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='logcosh')
    S=ica.fit_transform(X)
    k=kurtosis(S,axis=0)
    sk=(S**3).mean(0)
    o=np.argsort(-k)
    print('r',r,'kurt',k[o].round(0)); print('   skew',sk[o].round(1))
# raw distribution of how large projection is vs bulk
X=(u[:,:32].T@P).T
print('column norm in top32 pct',np.percentile(np.linalg.norm(X,axis=1),[5,25,50,75,95,99,100]))

```

## Call 16

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage,leaves_list
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T@Z
tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
Y=u[:,:30]
wl=W['ln1_w.3']
for h in range(6):
    O=W['W_o.3'][:,64*h:64*h+64]@W['W_v.3'][64*h:64*h+64]@np.diag(wl)
    c=O@tvx
    print('L3H',h,'W_o energy in Y',round(float(np.linalg.norm(Y.T@W['W_o.3'][:,64*h:64*h+64])**2/np.linalg.norm(W['W_o.3'][:,64*h:64*h+64])**2),3),'token->out energy in Y',round(float(np.linalg.norm(Y.T@c)**2/np.linalg.norm(c)**2),3),'sv',np.linalg.svd(Y.T@c,compute_uv=False)[:5])
for l in range(4):
    PF=W[f'W_proj.{l}']@W[f'W_fc.{l}']@np.diag(W[f'ln2_w.{l}'])
    c=Y.T@PF@tvx
    print('MLP layer',l,'token->Y fro',round(float(np.linalg.norm(c)),2),'sv',np.linalg.svd(c,compute_uv=False)[:6])
# linear token -> Y map through MLP 3
PF=P@W['W_fc.3']@np.diag(W['ln2_w.3'])
yc=Y.T@PF@tvx
yc=yc-yc.mean(1,keepdims=True)
Nrm=yc/np.linalg.norm(yc,axis=0,keepdims=True)
C=Nrm.T@Nrm
L=leaves_list(linkage(Nrm.T,'average',metric='cosine'))
print('order',L)
print((C[np.ix_(L,L)]*10).round(0).astype(int))

```

## Call 17

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T@Z
tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
Y=u[:,:30]
def out(l,h,w=None):
    wl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*h:64*h+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*h:64*h+64]@np.diag(wl)
ys={}
for l in [2,3]:
    for h in range(6):
        c=Y.T@out(l,h)@tvx
        c=c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
        ys[(l,h)]=c
        print((l,h),'sv',np.linalg.svd(c,compute_uv=False)[:27].round(1))
print('per-token cosine between token->output maps of pairs of heads')
keys=[(3,1),(3,5),(3,0),(3,2),(3,3),(3,4)]
for a in range(len(keys)):
    for b in range(a+1,len(keys)):
        A=ys[keys[a]];C=ys[keys[b]]
        cs=(A*C).sum(0)/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)/np.linalg.norm(C,axis=0)
        print(keys[a],keys[b],'mean cos',cs.mean().round(3),'min',cs.min().round(2),'max',cs.max().round(2))
A=ys[(3,1)];C=ys[(3,5)]
cs=(A*C).sum(0)/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)/np.linalg.norm(C,axis=0)
print('per token (3,1) vs (3,5)',cs.round(2))
print('norm per token (3,1)',np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0).round(1));print('norm per token (3,5)',np.linalg.norm(C,axis=0).round(1))
# Gram of output patterns for the H5 map, compare to same-token identity
Na=A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0);Nc=C/np.linalg.norm(C,axis=0)
X=Na.T@Nc
print('diag mean',np.diag(X).mean().round(3),'offdiag rms',np.sqrt(((X-np.diag(np.diag(X)))**2).sum()/(27*26)).round(3))

```

## Call 18

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T@Z
tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
Y=u[:,:30]
def cout(l,h):
    c=Y.T@W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*h:64*h+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*h:64*h+64]@np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])@tvx
    return c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
A=cout(3,1);C=cout(3,5)
oc=A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0)+C/np.linalg.norm(C,axis=0)
oc=oc-oc.mean(1,keepdims=True); oc=oc/np.linalg.norm(oc,axis=0)
for l in [3,2,1,0]:
    PF=W[f'W_proj.{l}']@W[f'W_fc.{l}']@np.diag(W[f'ln2_w.{l}'])
    yc=Y.T@PF@tvx
    yc=yc-yc.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    BG=oc.T@yc              # rows: next token, cols: current token
    BG=BG-BG.mean(0,keepdims=True)
    BG=BG/BG.std()
    print('layer',l,'corr with vowel-next: rows=next'); 
    vow=[2,6,7,13,5]
    print(' mean(next in vowels) per current token',BG[vow].mean(0).round(1))
    print(' top next tokens per current token:')
    for c in range(27):
        o_=np.argsort(-BG[:,c])[:5]
        print('  cur',c,'->',[(int(i),round(float(BG[i,c]),1)) for i in o_])
```

## Call 19

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage,leaves_list,fcluster
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
print('token idx   ',np.arange(27))
for j in range(6):
    V=W['W_v.0'][64*j:64*j+64]
    a=V@tv; 
    a=a-a.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    uu,ss,vv=np.linalg.svd(a,full_matrices=False)
    print(f'V H{j} sv',ss[:7])
    for i in range(3 if j==4 else 2):
        print('   comp',i,(vv[i]*ss[i]))
    print('   per-token V norm',np.linalg.norm(a,axis=0))
# token features from all L0 Q,K,V
F=[]
for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
    F.append(W[f'{nm}.0']@tv)
F=np.vstack(F); F=F-F.mean(1,keepdims=True)
N=F/np.linalg.norm(F,axis=0)
C=N.T@N
L=leaves_list(linkage(N.T,'average',metric='cosine'))
print('order',L); print((C[np.ix_(L,L)]*10).round(0).astype(int))
for nc in [2,3,4,6,8]:
    print(nc,fcluster(linkage(N.T,'average',metric='cosine'),nc,'maxclust'))

```

## Call 20

```python
import numpy as np
from collections import Counter
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]
A=(R-R.mean(0))@Z
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
Fw=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2)
A5=Fw@Oh(0,5)@T@Z; A1=Fw@Oh(0,1)@T@Z
A5=A5-A5.mean(0);A1=A1-A1.mean(0)
zc=A/A.std(0); z5=A5/A5.std(0); z1=A1/A1.std(0)
print('neuron selectivity: how many neurons exceed thresholds')
for th in [3,4,5,6]:
    print(th,'cur',(zc.max(1)>th).sum(),'prev',(z5.max(1)>th).sum(),'back2',(z1.max(1)>th).sum(),'cur&prev',((zc.max(1)>th)&(z5.max(1)>th)).sum(),'prev&back2',((z5.max(1)>th)&(z1.max(1)>th)).sum())
# what is strongest for each neuron: which slot wins
win=np.argmax(np.c_[zc.max(1),z5.max(1),z1.max(1)],axis=1)
print('win slot counts cur/prev/back2',np.bincount(win))
pairs=Counter();
for n in range(1536):
    c=zc[n].argmax();p=z5[n].argmax()
    if zc[n,c]>3 and z5[n,p]>3: pairs[(p,c)]+=1
print('n conj prev,cur',sum(pairs.values()),pairs.most_common(50))
print('count per token as cur (zc>4)',(zc>4).sum(0));print('count per token as prev (z5>4)',(z5>4).sum(0));print('count per token as back2 (z1>4)',(z1>4).sum(0))
# neg selectivity
print('neg count cur',(zc<-3).sum(0));print('neg count prev',(z5<-3).sum(0))
```

## Call 21

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
w2=W['ln2_w.0']
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
def B(l,j):
    Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
    return Q.T@K
# real negative modes: right (key side) and left (query side) eigenvectors of each head
for j in [1,5,0,3,2]:
    b=B(0,j)
    ev,VR=np.linalg.eig(b); evl,VL=np.linalg.eig(b.T)
    i=np.argmax((np.abs(ev.imag)<1e-6)*(ev.real<0)*np.abs(ev)); il=np.argmin(np.abs(evl-ev[i]))
    vr=VR[:,i].real; vl=VL[:,il].real
    vr/=np.linalg.norm(vr); vl/=np.linalg.norm(vl)
    print(f'H{j} lam {ev[i].real:.2f}  key side dot tokens',(vr@tv).round(2))
    print(f'    query side dot tokens',(vl@tv).round(2))
    print('    corr with ln1_w',np.corrcoef(np.abs(vr),w1)[0,1].round(2),np.corrcoef(np.abs(vl),w1)[0,1].round(2),' cos(vr,w1)',round(float(vr@w1/np.linalg.norm(w1)),3),' cos(vl,w1)',round(float(vl@w1/np.linalg.norm(w1)),3),' T-energy key',round(float(np.linalg.norm(T.T@vr)**2),2),'query',round(float(np.linalg.norm(T.T@vl)**2),2))
    if j==1: v1r,v1l=vr,vl
    else: print('    overlap with H1 mode key',round(float(abs(vr@v1r)),2),'query',round(float(abs(vl@v1l)),2))
```

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']

F=W['W_fc.0']
u,sf,vt=np.linalg.svd(F,full_matrices=False)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
def orth(A,r):
    return np.linalg.svd(A,full_matrices=False)[0][:,:r]
subs={'cur':orth(np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T,30)}
for j in range(6): subs[f'H{j}']=orth(np.diag(w2)@Oh(0,j)@T,28)
pos=np.linalg.svd(np.vstack([W['W_q.0'][64:128],W['W_k.0'][64:128],W['W_q.0'][320:384],W['W_k.0'][320:384]]),full_matrices=False)[2][:40].T
subs['pos']=orth(np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@pos,40)
for r in [90,130,180]:
    X=F@vt[:r].T
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=6000,tol=1e-4,random_state=0,fun='cube')
    S=ica.fit_transform(X)
    k=kurtosis(S,axis=0)
    D=vt[:r].T@ica.components_.T
    D=D/np.linalg.norm(D,axis=0)
    names=list(subs)
    frac=np.array([[np.linalg.norm(subs[n].T@D[:,c])**2 for n in names] for c in range(r)])
    best=frac.argmax(1); val=frac.max(1)
    print('r',r,'median best frac',np.median(val).round(2))
    for th in [0.3,0.5]:
        print(' assign counts at frac>',th,{names[i]:int(((best==i)&(val>th)).sum()) for i in range(len(names))},'unassigned',int((val<=th).sum()))
    # top neuron share
    S2=S**2
    print(' neurons per comp (participation)',np.percentile((S2.sum(0)**2/(S2**2).sum(0)),[10,50,90]).round(1))
```

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv
tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
slots={'c':tvx,'p':Oh(0,5)@tv,'b':Oh(0,1)@tv}
def zdiag(M):
    M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True)-M.mean(1,keepdims=True)+M.mean()
    d=np.diag(M); off=M[~np.eye(27,dtype=bool)]
    return (d.mean()-off.mean())/(off.std()/np.sqrt(27))
print('z of same-token advantage (query slot>key slot). c=current p=prev(L0H5) b=2-3 back(L0H1)')
pairs=[('c','c'),('c','p'),('c','b'),('p','c'),('p','p'),('p','b'),('b','c'),('b','p'),('b','b')]
print('       '+' '.join(f'{a}>{b:<4}' for a,b in pairs))
res={}
for l in [1,2,3]:
    wl=np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64];K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
        row=[]
        for a,b in pairs:
            M=(Q@wl@slots[a]).T@(K@wl@slots[b])
            row.append(zdiag(M))
        res[(l,j)]=row
        print(f'L{l}H{j} '+' '.join(f'{v:+6.1f}' for v in row))
# output-side: similarity of each head's token map to the L3H1/L3H5 copy map
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False);Y=u[:,:30]
def cout(l,h):
    c=Y.T@Oh(l,h)@np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])@tvx
    return c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
ref=cout(3,1)/np.linalg.norm(cout(3,1),axis=0)+cout(3,5)/np.linalg.norm(cout(3,5),axis=0)
for l in [1,2,3]:
    print('L',l,'cos to copy map',[round(float(((cout(l,h)*ref).sum(0)/np.linalg.norm(cout(l,h),axis=0)/np.linalg.norm(ref,axis=0)).mean()),2) for h in range(6)])
```

## Call 25

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0']
def B(l,j,wl=None):
    Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
    return Q.T@K
# position modes from L0H5 + L0H1 joint subspace, in h0 coords
M=np.vstack([W['W_q.0'][64:128],W['W_k.0'][64:128],W['W_q.0'][320:384],W['W_k.0'][320:384]])
u,s,vt=np.linalg.svd(M,full_matrices=False)
E=vt[:69].T
b5=E.T@B(0,5)@E
ev,V=np.linalg.eig(b5); o=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev)); ev=ev[o];V=V[:,o]
modes=[]
for i in range(30):
    if ev[i].imag<-1e-9: continue
    if abs(ev[i])<1.0: continue
    modes.append((ev[i],E@V[:,i]))
om=np.array([np.degrees(np.angle(m[0])) for m in modes])
print('mode freq (deg)',om.round(0),'n',len(om))
ds=np.arange(0,13)
def kern(gs,om,ds,mask=None):
    # score at offset d from per-mode complex gain
    out=[]
    for d in ds:
        out.append(np.sum(np.abs(gs)*np.cos(np.radians(om*d)-np.angle(gs))))
    return np.array(out)
print('offset          ',ds)
for j in range(6):
    g=np.array([ (m[1].conj()@B(0,j)@m[1]) for m in modes])
    print(f'L0H{j} max|g|={np.abs(g).max():6.2f} ',kern(g,om,ds).round(1))
for l in [1,2,3]:
    wl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    for j in range(6):
        g=[]
        for ev_,v in modes:
            vx=(v/np.maximum(w1,0.05))*wl
            vx=vx/np.linalg.norm(vx)
            g.append(vx.conj()@B(l,j)@vx)
        g=np.array(g)
        print(f'L{l}H{j} max|g|={np.abs(g).max():6.2f} ',kern(g,om,ds).round(1))
```

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0'])
r=30; T=Vt[:r].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0)
Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
Uo,So,Vo=np.linalg.svd(W['W_o.0'])
Up,Sp,Vp=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.0'],full_matrices=False)
print('tokens           ',np.arange(27))
for l,nm in [(1,'W_k'),(1,'W_q'),(2,'W_k'),(3,'W_q')]:
    u,sv,vt=np.linalg.svd(W[f'{nm}.{l}'])
    wl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    print('---',nm,l,'sv',sv[:4])
    for i in range(3):
        d=vt[i]
        dx=d*wl  # read-out weights in x space
        dx=dx/np.linalg.norm(dx)
        print(f' dir{i}: overlap W_o.0 top dirs',np.abs(Uo[:,:4].T@dx).round(2),' W_proj.0 top',np.abs(Up[:,:4].T@dx).round(2))
        print('    cur ',(dx@tvx).round(1))
        for h in [5,1,4,0]:
            print(f'    H{h}  ',(dx@Oh(0,h)@tv).round(1))
```

```python
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        ls=len(line)-len(line.lstrip(' '))
        body=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',line[ls:]); body=re.sub(r' +',' ',body).rstrip()
        out.append(' '*ls+body)
    return '\n'.join(out)
voc="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"; ix={c:i for i,c in enumerate(voc)}
def simmat(txt):
    t=norm(txt); N=28; C=np.ones((N,N))*0.1
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]): C[ix[a],ix[b]]+=1
    pa=C.sum(1,keepdims=True)/C.sum(); pb=C.sum(0,keepdims=True)/C.sum()
    pmi=np.log((C/C.sum())/(pa*pb))
    F=np.hstack([pmi,pmi.T])
    F=F-F.mean(0)
    n=np.linalg.norm(F,axis=1,keepdims=True)
    return (F@F.T)/(n@n.T),C.sum(1)
# token Gram from the weights
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
F_=np.vstack([W[f'{n}.0']@tv for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']]); F_=F_-F_.mean(1,keepdims=True)
N_=F_/np.linalg.norm(F_,axis=0); G=N_.T@N_
iu=np.triu_indices(27,1)
g=G[iu]; g=(g-g.mean())/g.std()
def anneal(Sm,seed,iters=200000):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=Sm.shape[0]
    p=np.concatenate([[ix[' ']],rng.permutation([i for i in range(n) if i!=ix[' ']])]) # p[0]=space placeholder
    # p maps token index -> char; force token 23 -> space
    p=list(p); p[0],p[23]=p[23],p[0]
    p=np.array(p)  # length 28, last slot unused
    def score(p):
        s=Sm[np.ix_(p[:27],p[:27])][iu]
        return np.corrcoef(g,s)[0,1]
    cur=score(p);best=cur;bp=p.copy();Tm=0.02
    for it in range(iters):
        a=rng.integers(27)
        if a==23: continue
        b=rng.integers(28)
        if b==23 or a==b: continue
        p[a],p[b]=p[b],p[a]
        sc=score(p)
        if sc>cur or rng.random()<np.exp((sc-cur)/Tm): cur=sc
        else: p[a],p[b]=p[b],p[a]
        if cur>best: best=cur;bp=p.copy()
        Tm=max(1e-4,Tm*0.99997)
    return best,bp
import time
for nm,txt in [('english',EN),('python',PY),('romaji',RO)]:
    Sm,cnt=simmat(txt)
    t0=time.time()
    res=[anneal(Sm,sd,120000) for sd in range(3)]

    b,p=res[0]
```

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, spearmanr
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
slots={'c':tvx,'p':Oh(0,5)@tv,'b':Oh(0,1)@tv}
print('tokens      ',np.arange(27))
rows={}
for l,j in [(2,1),(3,1),(3,5),(3,0),(2,2)]:
    wl=np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])
    Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64];K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
    for a,b in [('c','p'),('p','b'),('p','p'),('b','b')]:
        M=(Q@wl@slots[a]).T@(K@wl@slots[b])
        M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True)-M.mean(1,keepdims=True)+M.mean()
        d=np.diag(M); off=M[~np.eye(27,dtype=bool)]
        d=d/off.std()
        rows[(l,j,a,b)]=d
        print(f'L{l}H{j} {a}>{b}',d.round(0).astype(int))
keys=list(rows)
X=np.array([rows[k_] for k_ in keys if k_[:2] in [(2,1),(3,1),(3,5)]])
print('mean over copy heads',X.mean(0).round(1))
print('rank high->low',np.argsort(-X.mean(0)))
```

```python
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        ls=len(line)-len(line.lstrip(' '))
        body=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',line[ls:]); body=re.sub(r' +',' ',body).rstrip()
        out.append(' '*ls+body)
    return '\n'.join(out)
voc="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"; ix={c:i for i,c in enumerate(voc)}
def pmi(txt,k=2.0):
    t=norm(txt); N=28; C=np.zeros((N,N))
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]): C[ix[a],ix[b]]+=1
    pa=C.sum(1,keepdims=True)/C.sum(); pb=C.sum(0,keepdims=True)/C.sum()
    E=pa*pb*C.sum()
    return np.log((C+k)/(E+k))
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]
A=(R-R.mean(0))@Z
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
Fw=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2); A5=Fw@Oh(0,5)@T@Z; A5-=A5.mean(0)
zc=A/A.std(0); z5=A5/A5.std(0)
C=(z5.T@zc)/1536
mask=~np.eye(27,dtype=bool)
def run(Cm,Pm,seed,iters=60000):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=rng.permutation(28)
    sp=int(np.where(p==ix[' '])[0][0]); p[sp],p[23]=p[23],p[sp]
    y=Cm[mask]; y=(y-y.mean())/y.std()
    def sc(p):
        x=Pm[np.ix_(p[:27],p[:27])][mask]
        return (y*(x-x.mean())).mean()/x.std()
    cur=sc(p);best=cur;bp=p.copy();Tm=0.02
    for it in range(iters):
        a=rng.integers(27);b=rng.integers(28)
        if a==23 or b==23 or a==b: continue
        p[a],p[b]=p[b],p[a]
        s_=sc(p)
        if s_>cur or rng.random()<np.exp((s_-cur)/Tm): cur=s_
        else: p[a],p[b]=p[b],p[a]
        if cur>best: best=cur;bp=p.copy()
        Tm=max(2e-4,Tm*0.9998)
    return best,bp
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
for nm,txt in [('english',EN),('python',PY),('romaji',RO)]:
    Pm=pmi(txt)
    res=sorted([run(C,Pm,s_) for s_ in range(4)],key=lambda r:-r[0])
    nulls=[]
    for n in range(4):
        Cn=C.copy(); v=Cn[mask]; rng.shuffle(v); Cn[mask]=v
        nulls.append(run(Cn,Pm,100+n)[0])
    print(nm,'real best',round(res[0][0],3),[round(r[0],3) for r in res],'null',np.round(nulls,3))
    b,p=res[0]
    print('  ',{int(t):voc[p[t]].replace('\n','NL') for t in range(27)})
```

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
slots={'c':tvx,'p':Oh(0,5)@tv-(Oh(0,5)@tv).mean(1,keepdims=True),'b':Oh(0,1)@tv-(Oh(0,1)@tv).mean(1,keepdims=True)}
print('tokens           ',np.arange(27).astype(float).round(0))
for l in [1,2,3]:
    wl=np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])
    for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
        for sl in 'cpb':
            g=np.linalg.norm(W[f'{nm}.{l}']@wl@slots[sl],axis=0)
            print(f'L{l} {nm} {sl}',(g/np.median(g)).round(1))
# coordinates of copy output vectors on the big singular directions of W_proj.3
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False);Y=u[:,:30]
def cout(l,h):
    c=Y.T@Oh(l,h)@np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])@tvx
    return c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
A_=cout(3,1);C_=cout(3,5)
oc=A_/np.linalg.norm(A_,axis=0)+C_/np.linalg.norm(C_,axis=0)
print('copy vec on Wproj3 top dir 0',oc[0].round(2));print('on dir 1',oc[1].round(2));print('on dir 2',oc[2].round(2))
print('total energy on last dirs (index 24..29) per token',np.linalg.norm(oc[24:30],axis=0).round(2))
print('total energy on first dirs (0..5) per token',np.linalg.norm(oc[:6],axis=0).round(2))
```

## Call 33

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis, spearmanr
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=2,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
r=32;Y=u[:,:r]
def cout(l,h):
    c=Y.T@Oh(l,h)@np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])@tvx
    return c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
A_=cout(3,1);C_=cout(3,5)
for nm,oc in [('H1',A_),('H5',C_),('avg',A_/np.linalg.norm(A_,axis=0)+C_/np.linalg.norm(C_,axis=0))]:
    oc=oc/np.linalg.norm(oc,axis=0)
    gain=np.sqrt(((sp[:r,None]**2)*oc**2).sum(0))
    idx=((np.arange(r)[:,None])*oc**2).sum(0)
    print(nm,'gain',gain.round(1))
    print('   mean sing index',idx.round(1))
    print('   sorted tokens by gain high->low',np.argsort(-gain))
    print('   sorted gains',np.sort(gain)[::-1].round(1))
G=[]
for oc in [A_,C_]:
    oc=oc/np.linalg.norm(oc,axis=0)
    G.append(np.sqrt(((sp[:r,None]**2)*oc**2).sum(0)))
print('agreement H1 vs H5 gains spearman',spearmanr(G[0],G[1]).correlation)
# independent rarity proxies
slots_p=Oh(0,5)@tv; slots_p=slots_p-slots_p.mean(1,keepdims=True)
vH5=np.linalg.norm(W['W_v.0'][320:384]@tv-(W['W_v.0'][320:384]@tv).mean(1,keepdims=True),axis=0)
pgain=np.mean([np.linalg.norm(W[f'{n}.{l}']@np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])@slots_p,axis=0) for l in [1,2,3] for n in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']],axis=0)
print('prev-slot gain',pgain.round(1))
gm=(G[0]+G[1])/2
print('spearman output gain vs prev-slot gain',spearmanr(gm,pgain).correlation,' vs V_H5 norm',spearmanr(gm,vH5).correlation)
```

## Call 34

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False);Y=u[:,:32]
def cout(l,h):
    c=Y.T@Oh(l,h)@np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])@tvx
    return c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
A_=cout(3,1);C_=cout(3,5)
oc=A_/np.linalg.norm(A_,axis=0)+C_/np.linalg.norm(C_,axis=0)
oc=oc-oc.mean(1,keepdims=True); oc=oc/np.linalg.norm(oc,axis=0)
BGs=[]
for l in range(4):
    PF=W[f'W_proj.{l}']@W[f'W_fc.{l}']@np.diag(W[f'ln2_w.{l}'])
    yc=Y.T@PF@tvx; yc=yc-yc.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    B=oc.T@yc
    B=B-B.mean(0,keepdims=True)-B.mean(1,keepdims=True)+B.mean()
    BGs.append(B/B.std())
BGs=np.array(BGs)
print('pairwise corr between layer tables',np.round(np.corrcoef(BGs.reshape(4,-1)),2))
Sm=BGs.sum(0)/2
idx=np.dstack(np.unravel_index(np.argsort(-Sm.ravel()),Sm.shape))[0]
print('top cur->next by sum over layers (z)')
print([(int(c),int(n),round(float(Sm[n,c]),1)) for n,c in idx[:45]])
print('bottom',[(int(c),int(n),round(float(Sm[n,c]),1)) for n,c in idx[-15:]])
print('max possible by noise: rms',Sm.std().round(2),'max',Sm.max().round(2))
print('per-layer values for 15->5',BGs[:,5,15].round(1))
# null: random per-layer row+col permutations
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
mx=[]
for t in range(500):
    Sn=np.zeros((27,27))
    for l in range(4):
        pr=rng.permutation(27);pc=rng.permutation(27)
        Sn+=BGs[l][np.ix_(pr,pc)]
    mx.append((Sn/2).max())
print('null max of sum: mean',np.mean(mx).round(2),'99pct',np.percentile(mx,99).round(2))
# also through the copy-free L0 path to prev slot: sum tables for prev token -> next token

```

```python
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        ls=len(line)-len(line.lstrip(' '))
        body=re.sub(r'[^a-z ]',' ',line[ls:]); body=re.sub(r' +',' ',body).rstrip()
        out.append(' '*ls+body)
    return '\n'.join(out)
voc="\n abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"; ix={c:i for i,c in enumerate(voc)}
def pmi(txt,k=2.0):
    t=norm(txt); N=28; C=np.zeros((N,N))
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]): C[ix[a],ix[b]]+=1
    pa=C.sum(1,keepdims=True)/C.sum(); pb=C.sum(0,keepdims=True)/C.sum()
    E=pa*pb*C.sum()
    return np.log((C+k)/(E+k))
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False);Y=u[:,:32]
def cout(l,h):
    c=Y.T@Oh(l,h)@np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])@tvx
    return c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
A_=cout(3,1);C_=cout(3,5)
oc=A_/np.linalg.norm(A_,axis=0)+C_/np.linalg.norm(C_,axis=0)
oc=oc-oc.mean(1,keepdims=True); oc=oc/np.linalg.norm(oc,axis=0)
BGs=[]
for l in range(4):
    PF=W[f'W_proj.{l}']@W[f'W_fc.{l}']@np.diag(W[f'ln2_w.{l}'])
    yc=Y.T@PF@tvx; yc=yc-yc.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    B_=oc.T@yc
    B_=B_-B_.mean(0,keepdims=True)-B_.mean(1,keepdims=True)+B_.mean()
    BGs.append(B_/B_.std())
Sm=np.sum(BGs,axis=0)/2      # rows next, cols cur
Tb=Sm.T                      # rows cur, cols next
def run(Cm,Pm,seed,iters=50000):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=rng.permutation(28)
    sp_=int(np.where(p==ix[' '])[0][0]); p[sp_],p[23]=p[23],p[sp_]
    y=Cm.ravel(); y=(y-y.mean())/y.std()
    def sc(p):
        x=Pm[np.ix_(p[:27],p[:27])].ravel()
        return (y*(x-x.mean())).mean()/x.std()
    cur=sc(p);best=cur;bp=p.copy();Tm=0.02
    for it in range(iters):
        a=rng.integers(27);b=rng.integers(28)
        if a==23 or b==23 or a==b: continue
        p[a],p[b]=p[b],p[a]
        s_=sc(p)
        if s_>cur or rng.random()<np.exp((s_-cur)/Tm): cur=s_
        else: p[a],p[b]=p[b],p[a]
        if cur>best: best=cur;bp=p.copy()
        Tm=max(2e-4,Tm*0.9998)
    return best,bp
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
for nm,txt in [('english',EN),('python',PY),('romaji',RO)]:
    Pm=pmi(txt)
    res=sorted([run(Tb,Pm,s_) for s_ in range(5)],key=lambda r:-r[0])
    nulls=[]
    for n in range(5):
        v=Tb.ravel().copy(); rng.shuffle(v)
        nulls.append(run(v.reshape(27,27),Pm,100+n)[0])
    print(nm,'real',[round(float(r[0]),3) for r in res],'null',np.round(nulls,3))
    b,p=res[0]
    print('  ',{int(t):voc[p[t]].replace('\n','NL') for t in range(27)})
```

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
print('tokens  ',np.arange(27).astype(float))
tabs=[]
for l in range(4):
    PF=W[f'W_proj.{l}']@W[f'W_fc.{l}']@np.diag(W[f'ln2_w.{l}'])
    c=u[:,:8].T@PF@tvx
    c=c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True); c=c/c.std(1,keepdims=True)
    tabs.append(c)
tabs=np.array(tabs)
for d in range(4):
    print('Y dir',d,'layers corr',np.round(np.corrcoef(tabs[:,d])[np.triu_indices(4,1)],2))
    for l in range(4): print(f'  L{l}',tabs[l,d].round(1))
    print('  sum',(tabs[:,d].sum(0)/2).round(1))
```

## Call 39

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from scipy.stats import kurtosis
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=250,precision=1,suppress=True)
w1=W['ln1_w.0'];w2=W['ln2_w.0']
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
r=30;Y=u[:,:r]
def cout(l,h):
    c=Y.T@Oh(l,h)@np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])@tvx
    return c-c.mean(1,keepdims=True)
A_=cout(3,1);C_=cout(3,5)
oc=A_/np.linalg.norm(A_,axis=0)+C_/np.linalg.norm(C_,axis=0)
letters=[i for i in range(27) if i!=23]
Q_,_=np.linalg.qr(oc[:,letters])
Res=np.eye(r)-Q_@Q_.T
# weight-of-W_proj.3 energy left in the complement, per coordinate
E=np.diag(sp[:r])@Res
uu,ss,vv=np.linalg.svd(E)
print('complement dims sv',ss[:8])
print('residual direction 0 in Y coords (first 8 sing dirs)',(vv[0][:8]*10).round(1))
print('tokens',np.arange(27).astype(float))
for name,d in [('res0',Y@vv[0]),('res1',Y@vv[1]),('res2',Y@vv[2])]:
    tabs=[]
    for l in range(4):
        PF=W[f'W_proj.{l}']@W[f'W_fc.{l}']@np.diag(W[f'ln2_w.{l}'])
        c=d@PF@tvx; c=c-c.mean(); c=c/c.std(); tabs.append(c)
    tabs=np.array(tabs)
    print(name,'layer corr',np.round(np.corrcoef(tabs)[np.triu_indices(4,1)],2))
    print('  sum',(tabs.sum(0)/2).round(1))
    print('  token 23 per layer',tabs[:,23].round(1))
    # does the copy-free space map exist? overlap with each head output of current-token
    print('  overlap of head token-23 output with dir (L3):',[round(float(d@Oh(3,h)@np.diag(W['ln1_w.3'])@tvx[:,23]/np.linalg.norm(Oh(3,h)@np.diag(W['ln1_w.3'])@tvx[:,23])),2) for h in range(6)])

```

```python
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.0']); T=Vt[:30].T
R=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(w2/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@T
ica=FastICA(n_components=30,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0,fun='cube')
S=ica.fit_transform(R)
k=kurtosis(S,axis=0); sg=np.sign((S**3).mean(0)); o=np.argsort(-k)
Um=(ica.components_.T*sg)[:,o]; Z0=Um/np.linalg.norm(Um,axis=0); Z=Z0[:,:27]; tv=T@Z
tvx=np.diag(1/np.maximum(w1,0.05))@tv; tvx=tvx-tvx.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def Oh(l,j): return W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]@W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
P=W['W_proj.3'];u,sp,vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
r=30;Y=u[:,:r]
Ms=[]
for l in range(4):
    PF=W[f'W_proj.{l}']@W[f'W_fc.{l}']@np.diag(W[f'ln2_w.{l}'])
    M=Y.T@PF@tvx; M=M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True); Ms.append(M/np.linalg.norm(M))
C=np.zeros((r,r))
for a in range(4):
    for b in range(a+1,4):
        C+=Ms[a]@Ms[b].T+Ms[b]@Ms[a].T
e,V=np.linalg.eigh(C); o_=np.argsort(-e); e=e[o_];V=V[:,o_]
print('consistency eigenvalues top',(e[:10]*100).round(1),' bottom',(e[-4:]*100).round(1))
# null: permute token columns independently per layer
rng=np.random.default_rng(0); nul=[]
for t in range(200):
    Ps=[m[:,rng.permutation(27)] for m in Ms]
    Cn=np.zeros((r,r))
    for a in range(4):
        for b in range(a+1,4): Cn+=Ps[a]@Ps[b].T+Ps[b]@Ps[a].T
    nul.append(np.linalg.eigvalsh(Cn)[-1])
print('null top eig 99pct',(np.percentile(nul,99)*100).round(1))
A_=Y.T@Oh(3,1)@np.diag(W['ln1_w.3'])@tvx;C_=Y.T@Oh(3,5)@np.diag(W['ln1_w.3'])@tvx
A_-=A_.mean(1,keepdims=True);C_-=C_.mean(1,keepdims=True)
oc=A_/np.linalg.norm(A_,axis=0)+C_/np.linalg.norm(C_,axis=0); oc=oc/np.linalg.norm(oc,axis=0)
print('tokens       ',np.arange(27).astype(float))
for i in range(6):
    d=V[:,i]
    prof=sum(d@m for m in Ms); prof=(prof-prof.mean())/prof.std()
    print(f'comp{i} eig {e[i]*100:.1f}  per-layer token corr',np.round(np.corrcoef([d@m for m in Ms])[np.triu_indices(4,1)],2))
    print('   cur token profile',prof.round(1))
    print('   copy-vector align',(d@oc*3).round(1))
    print('   sing-dir share top5',(d[:5]*10).round(0),' share in complement of letter copies',round(float(1-np.linalg.norm(np.linalg.qr(oc[:,[i_ for i_ in range(27) if i_!=23]])[0].T@d)**2),2))

```

## Call 41

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(linewidth=200,precision=2,suppress=True)
# scale of the attention score form after the LN weights, per head: larger = sharper, smaller = closer to uniform
print('head  |B|_F/8  top sv/8  cos(Q,K)  antisym/sym   V_fro  Wo_fro')
for l in range(4):
    w=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    for j in range(6):
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]
        V=W[f'W_v.{l}'][64*j:64*j+64]; O=W[f'W_o.{l}'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
        B=np.diag(w)@Q.T@K@np.diag(w)/8
        sv=np.linalg.svd(B,compute_uv=False)
        S=(B+B.T)/2;A=(B-B.T)/2
        print(f'L{l}H{j} {np.linalg.norm(B):7.2f} {sv[0]:7.2f}  {np.trace(Q.T@K)/(np.linalg.norm(Q)*np.linalg.norm(K)):+.3f}   {np.linalg.norm(A)/np.linalg.norm(S):.2f}   {np.linalg.norm(V):6.1f} {np.linalg.norm(O):6.1f}')

```
